## MAIS Manim v2 CameraFrame dt updater semantics

- Date: 2026-06-27
- Agent ID: A06
- Workstream: Visualization Lab and interactive math modules
- Status: Completed
- Objective: Continue source-level Manim fidelity by adding a pure dt-aware CameraFrame updater path that matches `camera.frame.add_updater(lambda frame, dt: ...)` accumulation.

- What changed:
  - Added `applyCameraFrameUpdatersWithDelta`, a pure helper that applies ambient CameraFrame rotation over the active intersection of `[elapsed - dt, elapsed]` and each updater's start/end window.
  - Preserved the existing elapsed-sampling `applyCameraFrameUpdaters` path used by CameraDirector and browser QA evidence.
  - Added regression coverage for frame-to-frame ambient rotation accumulation, including an interval that crosses the updater start time.
- TDD evidence:

  - GREEN: after adding the helper, stepping from 4s to 5s with `dt=1` applies only 30 degrees while landing on the same frame position as the existing absolute 5s sample.
- Checks run:

  - `npm run type-check` - passed.

- Checks not run:

  - No dev server/browser smoke was rerun because no Canvas/DOM rendering path changed.
- Assumptions:

## MAIS Manim v2 SVG compact command parsing

- Date: 2026-06-27
- Agent ID: A06

- Date: 2026-06-27
- Agent ID: A06
- Workstream: Visualization Lab and interactive math modules
- Status: Completed
- Objective: Replace the local removal/generic advanced-glyph behavior for California Visualization Lab entries with grade-appropriate, lab-theme-aligned K-G12 California lab metadata and card glyphs.

  - `components/visualizations/visualizationDiagnostics.test.ts`
  - `components/visualizations/visualizationLabPageRegressions.test.ts`
  - `coordination/session-logs/2026-06-27-A06.md`
- Plan:
  - Add RED catalog/page regressions for visible K-A.1, priority California template/formula fit, and lab-theme-derived tile glyphs.
  - Update the A06 catalog generation layer so California K/P1/P3-P5 and selected middle/high rows use age-appropriate templates and formulas.
  - Update the card logo glyph selection so K California labs no longer inherit sigma/pi/function/delta/infinity/radical index glyphs.
  - Run focused source tests, type-check if feasible, and a browser smoke for the California K visualization page.
- Scope confirmation:

  - Added California K lab display overrides for K-A.1 through K-F.1 so titles/focus/formulas are kindergarten-friendly and aligned to counting/cardinality, compose/decompose within 10, teen numbers, attributes/data, and shapes/position.
  - Added California grade-fit template/formula overrides for selected P1-P5, S2, S5, and S6 labs where generic heuristics had produced statistics, coordinate, calculus, or area language that did not match the grade/theme.
  - Tightened Grade 1 California micro-lab formulas and moved cube-train join/take-away labs onto number-line style metadata rather than area wording.
  - Replaced index-based advanced tile glyphs with lab-theme-derived glyphs, including K-specific `123`, `3>2`, `2+3`, `10+`, `sort`, and `shape`.

  - RED: `visualizationLabPageRegressions.test.ts` failed because tile logos still used the generic glyph cycle.

  - GREEN: All focused catalog/page tests passed after the metadata and glyph fixes.

  - `./node_modules/.bin/tsx --test --test-reporter=dot components/visualizations/visualizationDiagnostics.test.ts` - failed for the intended RED cases, then passed.
